# CF2 exception list (spec 0.3, CF2 and MG6; Linear N2), built from b6e64c1d.
#
# Each line is a site of the frozen engine (core paths and native component tree, FC6b) that holds
# knowledge refused by section 15.3: a physical role or the machinery behind it, a model, closure or
# equation of state, a named numerical method, scheme, limiter, interpolation, approximation,
# controller, field operator or boundary family, a route table keyed on such names, a primitive
# selected by its spelling, a physical constant, a case identifier, or a solve algorithm in the core
# paths (1.2, FC6).
#
#     <path>:<line> <term> <issue> | <excerpt>
#
# A site is one occurrence of a token that holds the term. <line> locates it (0 means the term is
# in the file path). <issue> is the Linear issue that takes the site over (OWNER_RULES in cf2.py).
# <excerpt> is the spelling of the token, string literals quoted; a line repeats when its token
# occurs more than once on it. test_cf2_architecture.py pairs the sites with this list by (path,
# term, excerpt), counted with multiplicity and whatever their lines: a new token fails, even where
# a site of the same term left the same file, and so does a listed site that has gone, while a
# re-wrapped or reformatted statement keeps its sites. Remove the line of a site you remove, in the
# same PR, or run
#
#     python -m tests.python.architecture.frozen_engine.refresh_exceptions cf2
#
# which never accepts a site the list does not hold. CI also scans the base of the PR and fails on
# any site that the base does not hold (refresh_exceptions.py --check-base), so editing this file
# by hand lets nothing in. The list was written at b6e64c1d, then written again at the same commit
# with the terms that the early audit of N2 added on 29/10, with the error-text fix and the solve
# algorithms of the independent review of N2 (30/10), and with one site per token occurrence after
# its follow-up review (30/10). G3 requires it to be empty (spec 0.3, section 1.5). The exit of
# Phase 1 removes the sites of the mesh route except those that G04, G05, G06, G10, G11 and N9 take
# over in Phase 2, and every site left names its Phase 2 issue (section 18.3, as amended on 29/10).
